When FP4 Tensor Cores have twice the throughput of FP8, Ozaki scheme II on FP4 theoretically achieves slightly higher performance than its FP8 counterpart. This paper further proposes kernel implementation optimizations raising the attained fraction of peak performance, obtaining a measured speedup on top of the theoretical advantage. We verify this on an RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell, achieving performance competitive with that of an existing FP8-based implementation of Ozaki scheme II, and actually exceeding it at a large problem size ($16384^3$).

</details>


### [89] [StateFlow: Sequence Pipeline Parallelism for Long-Context Modeling with Linear Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06838)
*Wenxuan Zhao,Yingfa Chen,Xu Han,Wenjing Han,Tianbo Huang,Zhiyu Li,Ao Sun,Jingheng Xu,Lin Gan,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: StateFlow是一种针对线性递归模型的序列流水线并行系统，通过将序列分块并调度执行、传播边界状态和梯度，减少激活生命周期以提升训练吞吐量，在32B参数、256K上下文的模型上实现了最高2.22倍吞吐量提升和2.45倍内存减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文训练对大型语言模型日益重要，线性注意力和状态空间模型虽能提升长上下文效率，但高效并行化递归和混合模型的长序列训练仍然具有挑战性。

Method: StateFlow将每个序列划分为多个块(chunks)并调度其执行，同时跨块传播边界状态和梯度，从而缩短激活生命周期。此外，采用基于profile引导的非均匀分块策略来平衡混合模型中的递归计算和softmax注意力计算，并将暴露有限并行性的状态转换计算与周围计算重叠。

Result: 在多达32B参数和256K上下文长度的模型上，StateFlow相比传统流水线并行实现了最高2.22倍吞吐量提升和2.45倍内存减少，使原本不可行的配置成为可能。

Conclusion: StateFlow通过序列流水线并行和profile引导的非均匀分块，有效解决了线性递归模型长序列训练中并行化效率低的问题，显著提升了吞吐量并降低了内存占用。

Abstract: Long-context training is increasingly important for large language models, and linear attention and state space models have become popular for improving long-context efficiency. However, efficiently parallelizing long-sequence training for recurrent and hybrid models remains challenging.
  We present StateFlow, a sequence pipeline parallelism system for models with linear recurrence. StateFlow partitions each sequence into chunks and schedules their execution while propagating boundary states and gradients across chunks, thereby reducing activation lifetimes and improving training throughput. StateFlow further uses profile-guided nonuniform chunking to balance recurrence and softmax attention computation in hybrid models, and overlaps state transitions that expose limited parallelism with surrounding computation. Applying StateFlow to models with up to 32B parameters and 256K context length, we achieve up to \(2.22\times\) throughput improvements and \(2.45\times\) memory reduction compared to conventional pipeline parallelism, enabling otherwise infeasible configurations.

</details>


### [90] [Stream Learning: Partition-Fair Gossip Learning Without Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06946)
*Fabien Mathieu,Alexandre Pham,Maria Gradinariu Potop-Butucaru,S{é}bastien Tixeuil*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Stream Learning的十种协议家族，用于gossip学习中的模型分区调度。通过类比P2P直播流媒体（模型分区视为视频块，分区年龄视为块稀缺度），设计了"先分区"或"先邻居"的两阶段选择策略。核心发现是最简单的协议Ri（传输本地训练最少的区块给随机邻居）在无故障情况下与PTGL相当，在30%节点永久崩溃的对抗性场景下匹配或超越PTGL，最高差距达5.53%（HAR）和5.41%（MNIST）在Dirichlet β=0.1的最异构配置下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最先进gossip学习协议PTGL依赖基于token的公平机制和逐邻居元数据交换来调度分区，但作者质疑这种复杂性是否必要。通过将模型分区调度问题类比为P2P直播流媒体中的块稀缺性问题，作者希望探索更简单的分区调度策略是否能够在降低复杂度的同时达到或超越PTGL的性能，尤其是在异构和节点故障场景下。

Method: 作者通过类比P2P直播流媒体（分区即视频块，分区年龄即块稀缺度），构建了一个两阶段选择策略的设计空间：（1）先选分区再选邻居，或（2）先选邻居再选分区。从该设计空间中实例化了十种具体的Stream Learning协议。其中最简单的Ri策略是：每个节点将本地训练最不充分的分区传输给一个均匀随机选择的邻居，无需token计数器或元数据交换。实验在异构数据分布（Dirichlet分布）和30%永久节点崩溃的对抗性场景下进行系统评估。

Result: Ri协议在无故障工作负载下与PTGL性能匹配。在30%最佳性能节点永久崩溃的对抗性场景下，Ri在所有完整的完全图配置中匹配或超越PTGL。在最异构的配置（Dirichlet β=0.1）中，Ri在HAR数据集上领先5.53%，在MNIST上领先5.41%。实验表明，基于单一局部规则（分区年龄）的分区公平性是产生性能差距的关键因素；基于token的速率控制和效用最大化并未优于这一简单规则，且在异构条件下表现更差。

Conclusion: 本文表明，gossip学习中复杂的分区调度机制（如token计数和元数据交换）并非必要。一个简单的局部规则——传输训练最不充分的分区给随机邻居——就能在正常条件下匹配PTGL，并在对抗性节点故障和异构数据分布下超越它。分区年龄所体现的分区公平性是性能的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: In gossip learning, a network of nodes trains a shared model collaboratively, without a central coordinator, by repeatedly exchanging parts of their local models. The state-of-the-art protocol, Partitioned Token Gossip Learning (PTGL) of Heged{ü}s et al., splits the weight matrix into S fixed partitions and disseminates them using a token-based fairness mechanism coupled with per-neighbor metadata exchange. We revisit partition scheduling by analogy with peer-to-peer live streaming, where model partitions act as video chunks and partition age acts as chunk scarcity. The analogy yields a design space of two-stage selection strategies (partition first, or neighbor first), from which we instantiate ten concrete protocols collectively called Stream Learning. Our main finding is that the simplest of these protocols, which transmits the locally least-trained partition to a uniformly random neighbor (Ri), matches PTGL on fault-free workloads while requiring neither token counters nor metadata exchange. Under an adversarial 30% permanent crash of the best-performing nodes, Ri matches or outperforms PTGL across all complete-graph configurations tested, with the gap reaching 5.53% on HAR and 5.41% on MNIST in the most heterogeneous regime (Dirichlet $β$ = 0.1). In our experiments, partition fairness, captured by a single local rule on partition age, accounts for the gap; token-based rate control and utility maximization do not improve over this rule and, under heterogeneity, sit below it.

</details>


### [91] [A Self-Adaptive Extensible CEP framework for the Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06966)
*Olaf Markus Link,Sukanya Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种具有负载调节功能的可扩展自适复杂事件处理(CEP)框架，使算子即使在过载情况下也能继续工作，适用于云边连续体中异构和资源受限的环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CEP系统在云边连续体中部署算子图时面临动态变化工作负载和异构节点间负载不均衡的挑战，可能导致个别算子因资源不足而过载，需要一种能实时检测和适应动态负载的框架。

Method: 提出一个具有负载调节功能的新颖可扩展自适应性CEP框架，该框架能够帮助算子在过载情况下继续运行，支持在异构和/或资源受限环境中部署算子。

Result: 论文提出了该框架的设计方案（具体实验验证结果在摘要中未详细提及）。

Conclusion: 该自适应性CEP框架能够应对云边连续体中动态变化的工作负载和不均衡的负载分布，提升算子在过载条件下的持续运行能力，从而促进算子在异构和资源有限环境中的部署。

Abstract: Traditional complex event processing (CEP) systems focus on extracting information out of simple events in the input event streams to detect complex event patterns. In the context of CEP, a computing unit that executes a complex data transformation task is called an operator. Typically, a CEP system consists of an operator graph that could be distributed over the cloud-edge continuum. However, an operator graph distributed over heterogeneous nodes comes with its own set of challenges. In fact, the nodes of an operator graph are typically subjected to dynamically changing workloads. Unbalanced load distributions across heterogeneous nodes may lead to processing overloads in individual operators that do not have enough resources to properly adapt to an increasing workload. As a result, a framework that can detect and adapt to dynamically changing workload in real time across the various nodes of the cloud-edge continuum becomes paramount. To this end, the goal of this work is to propose a new extensible self-adaptive CEP framework with load regulatory features that help operators continue working even in face of an overload, thus facilitating the deployment of operators in a heterogeneous and/or resource-limited environment.

</details>


### [92] [A Kubernetes Scheduler Plugin for Cluster-Wide Placement Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06987)
*Henrik Daniel Christensen,Saverio Giallorenzo,Jacopo Mauro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: OPSche是一个开源的Kubernetes Scheduling Framework插件，允许外部求解器与默认调度器协同驱动集群级放置决策，通过协调的框架钩子原子地验证和执行求解器生成的计划，支持三种触发模式和对应的阻塞变体，实验表明可提升最多3.0%的资源利用率并减少超过一秒的调度延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes默认调度器采用快速、局部放置决策的设计，导致资源碎片化、集群利用率降低和过度配置问题。外部求解器虽能计算全局放置计划，但在上游集群中强制执行这些计划很困难：Kubernetes不支持原生跨节点抢占，无协调的并发调度会导致不一致，而替换默认调度器将使部署与上游发布周期脱节。

Method: OPSche作为Kubernetes Scheduling Framework插件实现，通过协调的框架钩子原子地验证和执行求解器生成的放置计划，与默认调度器协同工作。支持三种触发模式：调度失败触发、周期性触发和待处理工作负载稳定时触发；每种模式还有阻塞变体，用于精细调控放置质量、延迟和中断的权衡。

Result: 与约束优化求解器配合使用，在广泛的集群配置中验证了可行性，报告显示资源利用率提升最高达3.0%，调度延迟降低超过一秒。

Conclusion: OPSche提供了一种无需替换默认调度器即可实现全局优化放置的方案，在保持与上游Kubernetes版本兼容的同时，提高了资源利用效率和调度性能。

Abstract: The default scheduler of Kubernetes, the state-of-the-art container orchestrator, uses fast, local placement decisions. Unfortunately, this design leads to resource fragmentation, reduced cluster usage, and overprovisioning. External solvers can compute global placement plans, but enforcing these plans in upstream clusters is hard. Kubernetes provides no native cross-node preemption, uncoordinated concurrent scheduling leads to inconsistencies, and replacing the default scheduler would sever deployments from upstream cycles. We present OPSche, an open-source Kubernetes Scheduling Framework plugin where external solvers can drive cluster-wide placement decisions in concert with the default scheduler. OPSche atomically validates and enforces solver-produced plans through coordinated framework hooks and supports three trigger modes: scheduling-failure, periodic, and stable-queue -- resp. triggered when a workload cannot be placed, at fixed time intervals, when the set of pending workloads stabilises. Each mode has a blocking variant for a finer tuning of placement quality, latency, and disruption. We pair OPSche with a constraint-based optimisation solver, showing its feasibility across a broad set of cluster configurations and reporting improvements of resource usage by up to 3.0% and scheduling latency by more than a second.

</details>


### [93] [HiSparse: Scaling Sparse-Attention Decoding with Hierarchical KV Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07009)
*Zhiqiang Xie,Zhangheng Huang,Tingwei Huang,Ziyi Xu,Ruiyang Ma,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HiSparse是一种精确的、与索引器无关的分层KV缓存方案，用于稀疏注意力长上下文LLM解码。它将请求的完整KV历史保留在主机内存中，用小而固定的GPU缓存限定解码内存足迹，通过融合CUDA内核在解码图内解析每层的命中检测、LRU替换和主机到设备取回，并利用层间共享选择进行精确预取来隐藏约一半的缺失开销。已合入SGLang上游，在三种稀疏注意力族及多种平台上最高提升峰值生成吞吐4.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力服务系统虽然每步解码只需读取少量KV条目，但KV缓存仍全部驻留在GPU HBM中，导致内存账单随完整上下文长度增长，解码在计算耗尽之前就遭遇容量墙，甚至超长上下文完全无法服务。需要一种方案突破GPU HBM容量限制。

Method: HiSparse采用分层KV缓存架构：将完整KV历史存于主机内存，GPU侧仅保留小且固定大小的缓存；设计融合CUDA内核在解码CUDA图内完成每层的选择解析（命中检测、LRU替换、主机到设备取回）；对层间共享选择（share selections across layers）的模型，实施精确的逐层预取，隐藏约一半的剩余缺失开销。由于仅改变KV的放置位置，模型输出完全不变。

Result: HiSparse已合并到SGLang上游，在H200、B200和GH200平台上对三种稀疏注意力族（DSA、NSA、Quest）进行评估：峰值生成吞吐量在长上下文工作负载中最高提升4.7倍，保持可比的每token延迟，并降低高负载下的首token时间。无IO的理想实验显示解析机制本身不增加可测量的每token开销，主机-设备IO是限驻留的唯一代价。

Conclusion: HiSparse通过将稀疏注意力解码的内存足迹与上下文长度解耦，有效突破了GPU HBM容量墙，在保持模型输出不变的条件下显著提升长上下文生成吞吐，并为稀疏注意力服务中受限驻留策略树立了新标杆。

Abstract: Top-k sparse attention makes long-context LLM decoding cheap to compute: each step reads only a few thousand selected KV entries rather than the full context. Serving systems, however, typically keep the entire KV cache in GPU HBM so that every position stays selectable, so a request's memory bill still grows with its full context length--decoding hits a capacity wall long before it runs out of compute, and a context whose KV cache exceeds HBM cannot be served at all. We present HiSparse, an exact, indexer-agnostic hierarchical KV cache for sparse-attention serving. HiSparse keeps each request's full KV history in host memory and bounds its decode footprint with a small, fixed-size GPU cache; a fused CUDA kernel resolves each layer's selections--hit detection, LRU replacement, and host-to-device fetches--inside the decode CUDA graph; and, for models that share selections across layers, exact layer-wise prefetching hides roughly half of the remaining miss overhead. Because only KV placement changes, model outputs are unchanged. HiSparse is merged into upstream SGLang and evaluated across three sparse-attention families (DSA, NSA, and Quest) on H200, B200, and GH200 platforms: it improves peak generation throughput by up to 4.7x on long-context workloads while preserving comparable per-token latency and reducing time-to-first-token at high load--and a no-IO oracle shows the resolution mechanism itself adds no measurable per-token cost, leaving host-device IO as the only price of bounded residency.

</details>


### [94] [Scalable High-Fidelity Macromolecular Docking for GPU-Accelerated Supercomputers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07078)
*Xiangyu Meng,Peng Chen,Mingzhen Li,Jianmin Wang,Sen Wang,Guangming Tan,Weile Jia,Mohamed Wahib,Tao Luo,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SparkleDock是一个基于GSO的可扩展柔性分子对接框架，通过重新设计GSO暴露细粒度并行性、将能量计算重构为Tensor Core兼容形式，并引入性能模型驱动调度实现负载均衡和跨GPU扩展，在单A100/H100 GPU上比LightDock快9.7倍和18.9倍，在512个GPU上实现两个数量级以上的加速，将对接时间从数小时缩短至秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性大分子对接在生物分子相互作用预测中具有高保真度，但大规模计算成本极高。现有的LightDock方法基于GSO具有较高的准确性，但存在并行度有限、计算不规则和严重的负载不均衡问题，无法在GPU超级计算机上高效执行。

Method: 1) 重新设计GSO算法，在中卫（glowworm）智能体层面暴露大规模细粒度并行性；2) 将主导性的能量评分计算重构为Tensor Core兼容的矩阵运算形式，通过结构化矩阵操作高效处理不规则成对交互；3) 引入性能模型驱动的调度机制，实现负载均衡和跨GPU的out-of-core扩展。

Result: 在单个A100和H100 GPU上分别获得9.7倍和18.9倍的加速比（相对于LightDock），在规模扩展时实现两个数量级以上的加速。在512个GPU上将对接时间从数小时降低到秒级，使得大规模高保真虚拟筛选从以前不切实际变为可行。

Conclusion: SparkleDock通过算法重构、计算核心优化和调度策略三方面的协同设计，成功解决了柔性对接在GPU超级计算机上的性能瓶颈，实现了近实时的柔性对接，为大规模高保真虚拟筛选开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: Flexible macromolecular docking offers high-fidelity predictions of biomolecular interactions, but remains prohibitively expensive at scale. Among existing approaches, LightDock leverages Glowworm Swarm Optimization (GSO) for accuracy, yet suffers from limited parallelism, irregular computation, and severe load imbalance, preventing efficient execution on GPU supercomputers. We present SparkleDock, a scalable GSO-based docking framework enabling near-real-time flexible docking. We redesign GSO to expose massive fine-grained parallelism at the glowworm-agent level, and restructure the dominant energy scoring computation into a Tensor Core-compatible formulation, enabling efficient execution of irregular pairwise interactions through structured matrix operations. We further introduce a performance-model-driven scheduling for load balancing and out-of-core scaling across GPUs. SparkleDock achieves 9.7 $\times$ and 18.9 $\times$ speedups over LightDock on single A100 and H100 GPU, and delivers over two orders of magnitude acceleration at scale. On 512 GPUs, it reduces docking time from hours to seconds, enabling large-scale, high-fidelity virtual screening previously impractical with flexible docking.

</details>


### [95] [Fast end-to-end cloud application cold-start with initscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07358)
*Ariel Szekely,Robert Morris,M. Frans Kaashoek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出initscripts机制，为开发者提供脚本化接口以声明无服务器函数的初始化流程，使云平台能够重叠setup与initialization阶段的执行，从而显著降低冷启动延迟（平均加速1.67X至1.81X）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器函数多为短时运行且频繁遭遇冷启动，启动延迟通常主导其执行延迟。启动延迟可分为setup（平台侧操作，如下载二进制、创建隔离环境）和initialization（应用侧操作，如连接服务、协调领取工作、下载输入）两部分，如何减少这两部分的总时间是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出initscripts机制：开发者通过脚本化接口向云平台声明应用的初始化例程，平台可以运行该initscript，通过将setup和initialization步骤重叠来降低应用启动延迟。应用运行起来后，initscript通过传递初始化结果来引导（bootstrap）应用完成启动。

Result: 使用initscripts，无需修改应用即可将ServerlessBench中若干Python应用的冷启动速度平均提升1.67X；服务器无服务器图像识别工作负载的启动时间提升1.81X；同时也加速了etcd和memcached等现成微服务的启动。

Conclusion: initscripts能够在不对应用做任何修改的情况下，通过重叠setup与initialization大幅加速无服务器应用的冷启动，且对现成微服务同样有效，是一种通用且高效的冷启动优化方案。

Abstract: Serverless functions are a popular way of deploying cloud applications. Because many of these functions are short- running and experience frequent cold-starts, start latencies often dominate their execution latency. Start latency can be broken down into two components: setup and initialization. Setup involves steps the cloud platform takes when starting an application, such as downloading its binary and creating an isolated execution environment. Initialization involves steps the application takes after it has started running but before it can do useful work, such as connecting to other services, coordinating to claim work, and downloading inputs.
  This paper contributes initscripts, which provide a script- able interface for developers to specify their application's initialization routine to the cloud platform. The platform can then run an application's initscript and reduce application start latency by overlapping setup and initialization steps. Once the application is up and running, the initscript bootstraps the application by transferring initialization results to it. Using initscripts, we were able to speed up cold-starts of several of the ServerlessBench Python applications by an average 1.67X with no modifications to the application. Initscripts also speed up start times for a serverless image recognition workload by 1.81X, and off-the-shelf microservices like etcd and memcached.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MSB-GFM，一个用于跨域多标签节点分类的图基础模型框架，通过多语义基元表示学习和语义-结构双通道架构解决现有图基础模型在多标签场景下的语义纠缠和跨域泛化不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型(GFMs)基于单标签假设，将每个节点嵌入为单一表示，对多标签节点而言这种表示本质上是用单点近似多个语义，导致语义纠缠和多标签判别困难；同时现有方法仅考虑同域场景，跨域泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出多语义基元表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点建模为语义基元的自适应组合，增强灵活的表征能力；同时开发语义-结构双通道架构并结合领域对抗训练，实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验验证了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过多语义基元表示和双通道对抗训练架构，有效解决了跨域多标签节点分类中的语义纠缠与领域迁移问题，为图基础模型在多标签场景下的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [97] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EntropyMoE是一种针对动态字节补丁设计的混合专家（MoE）架构，通过将补丁熵作为专家路由的坐标，替代传统字节级LLM中的均匀稠密前馈计算，实现了最低的留出每字节比特数并维持了相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字节级大语言模型虽然通过动态尺寸的补丁分组实现了无分词器建模，但所有补丁仍然使用相同的稠密前馈计算，无法根据补丁的语义和粒度差异自适应地分配模型容量。这种均匀计算限制了模型对动态字节补丁的表达能力。

Method: EntropyMoE将全局补丁Transformer中的稠密前馈模块替换为Top-K专家层。每个动态补丁作为专家路由的基本单元，其字节覆盖范围决定工作负载核算的贡献。路由器直接基于补丁熵选择专家，利用与动态补丁构建相同的粒度信号来组织稀疏计算；补丁熵和长度共同定义调节专家特化的特征空间。

Result: 实验表明，EntropyMoE在匹配的稠密和稀疏基线中取得了最低的留出每字节比特数，同时保持了可比的后续任务准确率。

Conclusion: 这项工作确立了补丁熵作为稀疏条件计算的有效路由坐标，将混合专家建模扩展到超越基于分词器表征的领域，为字节级LLM的能力自适应分配提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出贡献对比（CoCo）方法，用于解释稀疏Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）奖励模型的专家行为，通过选择路由权重和偏好判断联合差异最大的chosen-rejected响应配对来刻画专家角色，在自动和人工评估中优于基于路由、基于分数和基于稀疏自编码器的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE奖励模型的解释方法仅基于路由权重，只能揭示专家接收哪些提示（receives），无法反映专家如何评判响应（judges），对专家行为的刻画不完整。

Method: 提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）响应级解释方法：利用具有最大贡献差异的chosen-rejected响应配对，联合捕捉路由行为和偏好判断行为，从而更忠实地刻画专家的角色。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，CoCo生成的专家解释比基于路由、基于分数和基于稀疏自编码器的替代方法更连贯、更忠实、更专门化，同时保持具有竞争力的奖励建模精度。

Conclusion: 这是首个对MoE奖励模型解释方法进行系统研究的论文，证明了联合考虑路由与偏好判断的响应级解释方法能显著提升MoE奖励模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [99] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUCID是一种由大语言模型（LLM）引导的、可解释的、无需训练的无监督社区检测方法，通过四阶段流水线（初始化、合并、细化、选择）模拟自然系统中的相变动力学，利用LLM归纳的显式逻辑规则实现社区识别，并在真实数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于目标函数的社区检测方法在处理复杂图结构时效果有限，而深度学习方法虽然性能更优，但缺乏可解释性且依赖带标签数据和训练过程。作者希望利用LLM强大的推理能力和世界知识，实现既可解释又无需标签的社区检测。

Method: LUCID设计为四阶段流水线：（1）局部视角社区初始化：利用k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；（2）多因素社区合并：使用LLM归纳的规则迭代合并局部社区；（3）多粒度社区细化：并行应用LLM归纳的从粗到细规则以减少边界噪声；（4）全局视角社区选择：基于拓扑紧密度和边界清晰度识别高质量社区。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，作为无监督方法，LUCID达到了最先进的性能，并持续优于领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID验证了LLM在无监督社区检测任务中的有效性，通过将隐式知识转化为显式可解释的逻辑规则，同时兼顾了性能与可解释性，且完全不需要训练数据和标签。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [100] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出问题中心（issue-centric）的智能体自动设计范式，将修复进度作为显式的持久化问题状态来引导优化，并实例化为ADIAS框架，在五个交互式基准上平均超越最强基线25.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化智能体设计方法大多是候选为中心的（candidate-centric），跨轮次经验围绕候选智能体组织，导致修复进度隐式化。这引发三个问题：修复目标不精准、部分进度整合缓慢、无效干预在轮次间传播。

Method: 首先将问题中心智能体优化形式化，即修复进度作为显式持久化问题状态跨轮次传递以引导优化，而非每轮从候选历史重新推导。随后实例化为ADIAS框架，包含两个机制：(1) 持久化问题状态：维护稳定的问题身份、生命周期状态、支持证据以及干预-结果历史；(2) 问题引导优化：依据该状态联合提议修复目标和修订方向，进行聚焦的全代码修改。

Result: 在五个交互式基准上，ADIAS平均超越最强基线25.2%，并在四个骨干模型上取得一致增益。消融实验显示，移除持久化问题状态或以候选中心策略替代问题中心修订，性能下降最高达40.7%。

Conclusion: 问题中心的优化范式比候选中心范式更高效，持久化问题状态能有效引导智能体设计的迭代修复过程，显著提升自动化全代码智能体设计的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [101] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAP（Middle-layer Attention Prediction）方法，利用Question Contrastive Teacher Selection识别样本特定的教师层，并通过轻量级预测器蒸馏注意力，在进入语言模型层之前即完成视觉令牌剪枝，无需注意力图且兼容现有加速技术。在LLaVA-NeXT-7B的十个基准上，仅保留5.56%的视觉令牌即维持97.5%的性能，端到端加速3.09倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）处理大量视觉令牌导致计算开销巨大。现有基于注意力引导的视觉令牌剪枝方法存在两个问题：(1) 固定使用预定义中间层的注意力在样本间表现不稳定，因为不同样本对问题最敏感的层差异显著；(2) 获取中间层注意力需要在多个语言模型层中处理大量视觉令牌，此时已消耗大量计算。

Method: MAP包含两个核心组件：一是Question Contrastive Teacher Selection，通过对比原始问题与参考问题下的注意力分布来识别每个样本特定的最优教师层；二是将选定层的注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，该预测器从多模态输入特征中直接估计视觉令牌重要性。推理时将预测的重要性分数与多样性准则结合，在第一个语言模型层之前完成视觉令牌剪枝。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT-7B的十个基准上，MAP保留97.5%的未剪枝模型性能，仅使用5.56%的视觉令牌，实现3.09倍的端到端速度提升。

Conclusion: MAP通过样本自适应的教师层选择和轻量级注意力预测蒸馏，在不依赖推理时注意力图的前提下实现了高效的视觉令牌剪枝，有效解决了固定层剪枝的局限性和前期计算浪费问题，并与现有推理加速技术兼容。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [102] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebGrader是一个自进化的程序化奖励评分器，用于强化学习训练大型语言模型生成网站。它自动从网站请求中推导交互流程并表示为可执行的Flow Contract，在真实浏览器中执行以收集多维度证据，通过残差驱动的离线循环发现和筛选可复用的验证技能，最终为RL提供更准确的奖励信号。在WebGen-Bench上训练的8B策略达到52.01%的功能成功率，超越多种强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成完整网站时，强化学习训练受制于奖励设计瓶颈：手工编写的浏览器脚本可执行但编写成本高且难以应对开放性需求；基于VLM和GUI-agent的评分器虽可扩展，但可能在关键状态出现之前就给出判定，导致奖励信号不准确。

Method: WebGrader包含四个核心组件：(1) 从每个网站请求自动推导所需交互流程，表示为实现可执行的Flow Contract；(2) 在真实浏览器中实体化生成的项目，将目标动作锚定在源代码和实时DOM上；(3) 沿同一浏览器轨迹收集视觉、DOM、响应和持久状态证据；(4) 通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用的验证器技能，在不相交的验证页面上筛选，并在策略训练前冻结已晋升的技能图。该方法将测试规划、动作锚定、证据收集和语义判断分离，仅在观察到请求的状态转换后才给出Pass判定。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench上，WebGrader训练的8B策略达到52.01%的功能成功率，比匹配的外观加脚本奖励高7.88个百分点，并超越o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。在WG-core-250上，策略达到44.953的Full Score，超越Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: WebGrader通过将奖励设计从手工脚本或VLM猜测转化为自动推导、可执行、证据充分的程序化评分流程，有效缓解了RL训练中的奖励瓶颈，证明了高质量的自动化奖励信号能显著提升小规模策略在网站生成任务上的功能性表现。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [103] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C4——一个基于认知科学启发的成语跨概念创造力评估框架，包含编码（项目构建）和解码（模型推理）两个映射组件，构建了包含184个合成项和37个人工创建项的C4-Eval评估集，在10个MLLM上评估显示最强闭源模型主准确率约50%，开源模型显著更低，暴露了当前多模态大语言模型在跨概念创造性解码方面的明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的创造力在设计、沟通、教育和人机协作中至关重要，但已有评估方法因缺乏显式目标和奖励信号而难以实施。跨概念理解是接受性创造力的核心认知能力，使感知者能从非显而易见的但有意义的概念关系中恢复意图含义，但目前缺乏系统化的评估框架来衡量MLLM在这方面的表现。

Method: 作者将项目构建操作化为跨概念编码，将模型推理操作化为跨概念解码，引入C4框架。编码组件通过人工标注并经第三方审查的跨概念网络中的桥接路径，将目标槽映射到可成像的替代概念，实现具有显式结构（以桥接数量和深度为难度指标）和精确答案的批量生成。基于此框架构建C4-Eval评估集，包含184个合成项和37个人工收集的成语跨概念图形，每个条目在五种任务设置下实例化，共产生884个主答案恢复用例。

Result: 在10个评估的MLLM中，最强闭源模型的主准确率达到50.7%和48.0%，而开源模型则显著较低。候选约束能大幅提升准确率，但桥接提示和解释请求仅带来小幅提升。这一结果表明当前MLLM通过跨概念关系解码创造性编码意义的能力存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在跨概念创造性解码方面能力有限，尤其是在恢复通过非显而易见的概念关系编码的意义时表现不足。C4框架提供了结构明确、难度可控、答案精确的评估手段，为未来提升MLLM创造性解码能力提供了方向和基准。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [104] [KNOWPLAN: Knowledge-Driven AI Agents for Smart Degree Pathway Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06530)
*Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Xiyun Hu,Yat Yang,Juqy Chen,Zhaoxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowPlan是一个将课程目录抽取与学位路径规划解耦的两阶段系统。CatalogBrowse负责无用户档案的自主爬取与结构化抽取，DegreeMap仅基于抽取结果构建类型化需求超图并用CP-SAT进行词汇最优化。在100所大学的宽覆盖和6所学校的密集测试中，抽取召回率达96.2%，硬可行性100%，请求认证率99.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学位规划需要先重构学校课程体系（从异构来源如目录、部门页、JSON、PDF），再在前提逻辑和重叠需求约束下优化个性化路径。若将两阶段耦合，规划器驱动的爬取只会学习当前计划所需的事实，导致两类失败相互掩盖。因此需要强制抽取与规划解耦，并测量两阶段之间的接口质量而非假定其可靠。

Method: KnowPlan包含两个独立阶段：(1) CatalogBrowse：无用户档案探索，基于下置信期望边际增益对有限原子目录义务评分，通过平台适配器确定性解析，辅以跨度约束的子句到AST模型作为回退，以索引、模式、出处和引用完整性的闭包证书终止；(2) DegreeMap：仅消费三份带出处链接的JSON文档，编译为类型化需求超图，使用CP-SAT在硬可行性、完成时间、负载与风险、个性化效用和期权价值上进行词汇优化，保证每阶段在前一阶段的最优解内优化。

Result: 100所大学宽轨道和6所学校密集轨道上：CatalogBrowse达到96.2%库存召回率、88.7%掩码源恢复率，且源访问量比穷举爬虫少47%；DegreeMap保持100%硬可行性，个性化效用比最强基线提升+0.066；全流水线99.5%请求可认证，与特权金图的效用差距为0.015。

Conclusion: KnowPlan通过强制抽取-规划分离边界，证明了将课程目录重建与学位路径优化解耦既可行又高效。每个阶段在上一阶段已被证明的最优解内部运行，且保持可认证性，在实现高召回、高可行性和高认证率的同时大幅减少源访问开销。

Abstract: Planning a degree from official university sources requires solving two problems in order. The institution's curriculum must first be reconstructed from catalogs, departmental pages, JSON endpoints, and PDFs that share no schema, and only then can a student-specific path be optimized under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirement constraints. Coupling the two lets each failure mode hide the other, because a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. We present KnowPlan, which enforces an extraction-first boundary and measures the interface between the stages rather than assuming it. CatalogBrowse explores with no access to any user profile. It scores legal actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over a finite set of atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically through platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST model fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness instead of a reward threshold. Its output contract is three provenance-linked JSON documents. DegreeMap consumes only those documents. It compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph and optimizes lexicographically with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load and risk, personalized utility, and option value, so that each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum and stays certifiable within the solver budget. Across a 100-university broad track and a six-school dense track, CatalogBrowse reaches 96.2% inventory recall and 88.7% masked-source recovery at 47% less source access than an exhaustive crawler, DegreeMap holds 100.0% hard feasibility while improving personalized utility by +0.066 over the strongest baseline, and the full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a utility gap to the privileged gold graph of 0.015.

</details>


### [105] [TaskSense: Focusing on What Matters in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06544)
*SM Mazharul Islam,Manfred Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TaskSense是一个以任务为中心的世界建模框架，通过可微分的随机空间注意力机制和辅助逆动力学目标，确保潜在状态仅编码任务相关的视觉区域，而非重建全部观察，从而在视觉干扰环境下显著提升控制鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉控制世界模型（如DreamerV3）通过重建完整观察来学习紧凑潜在状态，隐式地要求表征保留整个视觉输入的信息。然而任务相关内容往往只占观察的一小部分，背景杂波和干扰物消耗了宝贵的表征容量。视觉重建目标与控制目标之间的不匹配导致潜在表征偏向建模任务无关内容，稀释了控制相关特征的学习信号，在视觉干扰下严重降低下游性能。

Method: TaskSense在潜在编码之前通过一个以先前潜在状态为条件的可微分随机空间注意力机制来强制任务相关性。通过辅助逆动力学目标引导注意力聚焦控制相关区域。世界模型仅重建被注意的区域而非完整观察，从而鼓励潜在表征保留任务相关信息并丢弃无关视觉内容。解码器进一步以采样的注意力图为条件，确保在随机注意力下仍能保持一致的重建。

Result: 与DreamerV3基线相比，TaskSense在DeepMind Control Suite上保持竞争力，同时在Distracting Control Suite上持续优于DreamerV3，展现出显著提升的视觉干扰鲁棒性。定性分析确认逆动力学监督引导的注意力能一致地定位控制相关区域并抑制无关视觉内容。

Conclusion: TaskSense通过将任务相关性注入注意力机制并在重建前过滤无关视觉内容，有效弥合了视觉重建与控制目标之间的差距，为在复杂视觉干扰环境下的视觉控制提供了一种更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: World models for visual control typically learn compact latent states by reconstructing observations, implicitly encouraging representations to preserve information across the entire visual input. However, task-relevant content often occupies only a small fraction of the observation, while background clutter and distractors consume valuable representational capacity. This mismatch between visual reconstruction and control objectives biases latent representations to model task-irrelevant visual content, diluting learning signals for control-relevant features and severely degrading downstream performance under visual distractions. We introduce TaskSense, a task-centric world modeling framework that enforces task relevance before latent encoding through a differentiable stochastic spatial attention mechanism conditioned on the previous latent state. To steer attention toward control-relevant regions, we augment training with an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective. Rather than reconstructing the full observation, the world model reconstructs only the attended regions, encouraging latent representations to preserve task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant visual content. The decoder is further conditioned on the sampled attention map, enabling consistent reconstruction despite stochastic attention. Compared with the DreamerV3 baseline, TaskSense maintains competitive performance on the DeepMind Control Suite while consistently outperforming DreamerV3 on the Distracting Control Suite, demonstrating substantially improved robustness to visual distractions. Qualitative analysis further confirms that the learned attention, guided by inverse-dynamics supervision, consistently localizes control-relevant regions while suppressing irrelevant visual content.

</details>


### [106] [FedLBW: A Loss-Based Weighting Strategy for Federated Learning on Non-IID Data in Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07007)
*Majid Kundroo,Tinku Singh,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedLBW（基于损失加权的联邦学习聚合算法），用服务器端小代理数据集上各客户端验证损失的倒数作为权重，替代传统基于数据集大小的加权方式，以应对无线网络中非IID数据和客户端掉线问题，在多个数据集上比FedAvg等基线算法精度提升高达7.6%，并表现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习算法（如FedAvg）仅依据数据集大小对客户端更新加权，这导致对大数据集客户端的偏置，对非IID数据、异常值和客户端掉线十分敏感，模型收敛效率低下。无线网络中非IID数据分布和频繁的客户端掉线使这一问题更加突出，迫切需要一种更鲁棒、更有效的聚合策略。

Method: FedLBW的核心思想是：服务器端使用一个小型代理数据集，计算每个客户端上传模型的验证损失，并以验证损失的倒数作为聚合权重，而非使用数据集大小。损失越低的模型在聚合时权重越大，从而优先采用最可靠的更新，提升整体聚合性能。

Result: 在FashionMNIST（CNN）、CIFAR-10（ResNet-18）和CIFAR-100（ResNet-34）多个数据集上，FedLBW相比FedAvg、FedAvgM、FedProx、FedNova、FedLAW和FedDkw等基线算法均取得更高的精度和更快的收敛速度，在CIFAR-10极端非IID场景下精度最高提升7.6%。此外，FedLBW在客户端掉线概率增加时仍能保持显著更高的精度，展示了优异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedLBW被验证为无线网络环境下联邦学习的一种有效且鲁棒的解决方案，在模型精度、收敛速度和面对非IID数据及客户端掉线的稳健性方面均有显著改进。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative machine learning (ML) across distributed clients while preserving privacy. However, efficient model convergence in FL remains challenging, especially in wireless networks where non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data and frequent client dropouts are common. Traditional FL algorithms, such as FedAvg, rely solely on dataset size to weight client updates. This introduces biases towards clients with larger datasets and makes the process sensitive to non-IID data, outliers, and client dropouts. To address these challenges, we propose Federated Learning with Loss-Based Weighting (FedLBW), a novel aggregation method that assigns each client's update a weight proportional to the inverse of its validation loss, computed using a small proxy dataset on the server, rather than its dataset size. This ensures that lower-loss models exert greater influence during aggregation, prioritizing the most reliable updates and boosting overall performance. Through extensive experiments across multiple datasets, including FashionMNIST (CNN), CIFAR-10 (ResNet-18), and CIFAR-100 (ResNet-34), we demonstrate that FedLBW achieves higher accuracy and faster convergence compared to baseline algorithms such as FedAvg, FedAvgM, FedProx, FedNova, FedLAW and FedDkw, with notable improvements of up to 7.6 % higher accuracy on CIFAR-10 in extreme non-IID cases. Moreover, FedLBW showcases exceptional resilience to increasing dropout probabilities, consistently maintaining significantly higher accuracy even in challenging conditions. These results establish FedLBW as an effective and resilient solution for FL in wireless network environments, offering marked improvements in model accuracy, convergence speed, and robustness to non-IID data and client dropouts.

</details>


### [107] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了六家开发商的前沿语言模型在显式转向压力下的行为可控性。通过300对基线和转向样本（涵盖价值观冲突、推理激发和推理抑制三类），24,480次盲审判断显示模型不仅在转向幅度上不同，在响应模式类型上也有显著差异，某些模式仅出现在个别模型中。通过线性探针和内部干预实验，研究进一步定位了Llama模型中行为分裂的内部机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管前沿语言模型在数据、目标和安全流程上接受不同的训练，但这些差异是否会在显式转向压力下产生可测量的行为差异这一问题尚未被充分探索。理解模型行为的可转向性差异对模型评估、对齐和安全研究至关重要。

Method: 选取来自六家开发商的六个前沿模型，使用300对配对基线和转向样本（覆盖价值观冲突、推理激发、推理抑制三类）外加40个验证样本。所有六个模型作为盲审评审员，基于固定行为评分标准对每个响应进行分类，产生24,480次判断，通过留一法共识评分。对Llama模型使用线性探针从残差流解码行为（0.87留出准确率），并注入该方向进行干预扫描。包含token预算补救和假设盲审控制实验。

Result: 模型不仅在转向后行为变化的幅度上有差异，在响应模式（mode）类型上也显著不同，某些响应模式仅存在于一个或两个模型中。GPT-5在拒绝披露推理过程的同时保留答案完整性（99% vs. 其余模型0%）。Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令。线性探针以0.87留出准确率从Llama残差流解码行为，注入该方向可将行为从0%驱动至86%。所有发现在token预算补救和假设盲审控制实验中均成立。

Conclusion: 前沿模型在转向压力下展现出具有开发者特异性的行为模式差异，而非仅存在转向幅度差异。这些差异可以追溯到模型的内部表征和机制层面，表明训练管线差异确实在模型的行为可塑性上产生了可测量的、可定位的后果。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models). Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5 resist explicit suppression instructions and in different ways. Using Llama as the open-weight model, we trace the largest behavioral split to its internals. A linear probe decodes the behavior from the residual stream at 0.87 held-out accuracy while injecting that direction during generation drives the behavior from 0% to 86% across an intervention sweep. Every finding holds under both a token-budget remediation and a control experiment with a hypothesis-blind judgment prompt.

</details>


### [108] [Automated item evaluation: Predicting item acceptance and rejection using LLM-generated critiques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06609)
*Hotaka Maeda,Yikai Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个基于文本的自动化条目评估（AIE）模型，利用大规模标准化测试项目中52,759个ELA和数学条目的历史拒绝数据，微调DeBERTaV3-large分类器及融合模型，实现对条目接受/拒绝的预测，融合模型达到Accuracy=.75、F1=.64、AUC=.80的最佳性能，其中数学预测优于ELA，且降低决策阈值可显著提升灵敏度，但对公平性相关条目的识别仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的条目质量评估依赖人工专家评审和现场测试，成本高、效率低。研究者希望通过文本驱动的自动化评估模型，在无需人工干预的情况下预测条目质量，从而减轻标准化测试项目中的评审负担。

Method: 使用52,759个ELA和数学条目的历史拒绝数据（34%被永久拒绝），微调两类DeBERTa模型：一个在原始条目文本上训练的DeBERTaV3-large分类器，一个在Qwen3生成的条目评论文本上训练的DeBERTa分类器，以及融合两者表示的融合模型。

Result: 融合模型表现最佳（Accuracy=.75, F1=.64, AUC=.80）。数学预测（F1=.73, AUC=.86）显著优于ELA（F1=.51, AUC=.72）。将决策阈值从.5降低至.25后，ELA和数学的平均灵敏度分别提升至.88和.91，但特异性降至.31和.56。融合模型对难度较高的条目赋予更高拒绝概率，但对偏倚/敏感性/公平性/可访问性问题识别困难，尤其是ELA。

Conclusion: 基于文本的AIE在部分领域是可行的，可作为减少人工评审和现场测试负担的实用工具；但对涉及公平性问题的条目，人工评审仍然不可或缺。

Abstract: Automated item evaluation (AIE) refers to the use of computational methods to assess item quality without requiring manual expert review or field testing of the items under evaluation. We aimed to build a near-comprehensive AIE model by predicting item acceptance and rejection from item text using historical rejection data from a large-scale standardized testing program. The dataset contained 52,759 English language arts (ELA) and mathematics items with 34% permanently rejected from future operational use. Rejection reasons included poor psychometric properties, content issues, bias and sensitivity concerns, and non-content issues. We fine-tuned a DeBERTaV3-large classifier on raw item text, a second DeBERTa classifier on Qwen3-generated item critiques, and a fusion model combining representations from both. The fusion model achieved the strongest overall performance (Accuracy = .75, F1 = .64, AUC = .80, Sensitivity = .64, Specificity = .81). Prediction for math (F1 = .73, AUC = .86) was considerably more accurate than ELA (F1 = .51, AUC = .72). Lowering the decision threshold from .5 to .25 raised average sensitivity for ELA and math to .88 and .91, while reducing specificity to .31 and .56, respectively, which may be preferable in automated item generation contexts where generating items is cheaper than evaluating them. Incorporating item critiques alongside raw item text improved performance across most rejection reasons. The model assigned higher rejection probabilities to more difficult items. However, the fusion model struggled to identify items flagged for bias, sensitivity, fairness, or accessibility, especially for ELA. These findings suggest that text-based AIE is feasible in some areas and may offer a practical tool for reducing the burden of manual review and field testing, while also underscoring the importance of human review for items with fairness concerns.

</details>


### [109] [NxN E-valuation: Hypothesis Certification via a Conformal CRT Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06621)
*Bin Wang,Yan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NxN E-valuation方法，一种基于e值的假设验证算法，利用大规模数据集中不同样本互为零假设，无需构建专用认证流程即可验证假设，特别适合LLM探索系统以缓解幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在探索系统中擅长提出假设但严重受幻觉问题困扰，直接使用LLM输出不可靠。现有解决方案（如循环验证、留出测试）各有缺陷——循环验证存在自我确认偏差，留出测试中虚假假设可能因虚假相关性而通过验证。

Method: 利用自然界存在的大规模训练集，让不同样本互为对方的零假设，直接实现条件随机化检验（CRT）来认证每个假设。该方法无需构建案例特定的认证程序（如构造专用零假设），只要有足够大的数据集即可工作。

Result: 在LLM生成是适用于每个单独样本的假设的前提下，该方法可以成为至少优于LLM循环验证和留出数据测试的普遍替代方案。

Conclusion: NxN E-valuation为LLM假设验证提供了一种便捷、无需特定场景定制的e值认证方案，有效缓解幻觉问题，适合大规模数据可用场景下的LLM探索系统。

Abstract: We propose NxN E-valuation, a handy, e-value-based hypothesis-certification algorithm that lets a hypothesis be verified without building any case-specific certification procedure---such as constructing a dedicated null hypothesis---as long as a large enough dataset is available. The method is especially suited to LLM-based exploration systems, where LLMs are remarkably good at proposing hypotheses but suffer badly from hallucination; this hallucination prevents us from harvesting LLM outputs directly, and existing remedies each fall short. The most common solutions include letting the LLM verify or correct itself circular verification and held-out testing (where false hypotheses can still pass via spurious correlations), among other remedies detailed in the introduction. To resolve this, NxN E-valuation exploits the naturally existing large training set and lets different samples serve as null hypotheses for one another. This design directly realizes a conditional randomization test (CRT) that certifies each hypothesis. The approach can be a universally better replacement for at least LLM circular verification and held-out-data testing, provided the LLM's generations are hypotheses that apply to each individual sample.

</details>


### [110] [Shape Your Feed: An LLM-based Agentic System for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06632)
*Ziyun Xu,Bosen Ding,Yue Zhang,Ji Qi,Qingyuan Song,Jizhou Huang,Liwei Wang,Jefferey Santelli,Yue Weng,Qichao Que,Zhenheng Yang,Junfeng Pan,Linhong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Shape Your Feed (SYF)，一个基于 LLM 的智能体推荐框架，旨在将工业推荐系统从被动排序范式转变为支持实时、多模态用户协同策展内容的交互式范式。三层架构包括感知流（Perception Flow）、服务流（Serving Flow）和自进化流（Self-Evolution Flow），离线评估中对齐评分模块达到 98.85% 准确率，大规模线上 A/B 实验证实其能提升推荐相关性和用户满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统普遍采用被动排序范式，仅从隐性行为信号（如点击、停留时长）推断用户偏好，而非利用显式的自然语言输入。这导致用户明确兴趣与算法交付内容之间存在持续性偏差，用户无法表达细微偏好或实时引导推荐流。作者旨在弥合推荐优化方式与用户表达意图方式之间的鸿沟。

Method: SYF 采用三层架构：(i) 感知流（Perception Flow）——从文本提示、语音指令和 UI 交互中捕获细粒度用户意图；(ii) 服务流（Serving Flow）——基于持久化语义画像（Semantic Profile）进行实时智能体重排序和候选项目剪枝；(iii) 自进化流（Self-Evolution Flow）——通过直接偏好优化（DPO）和 LLM-as-a-Judge 集成将系统行为与人类判断对齐。

Result: 离线评估显示 SYF 的对齐评分模块达到 98.85% 准确率，显著优于强少样本基线；大规模生产流量上的在线 A/B 实验表明 SYF 提升了推荐相关性和用户情感/满意度。

Conclusion: SYF 验证了交互式、用户可引导的推荐在工业环境中具有实用性和可扩展性，为推荐系统从被动优化向主动、可对话的协同策展演进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation systems predominantly adopt a passive ranking paradigm that infers user preferences from implicit behavioral signals (e.g., clicks, dwell time) rather than explicit, natural language inputs. As a result, users experience a persistent discrepancy between their explicit interests and what passive behavioral algorithms deliver, limiting their ability to express nuanced preferences or steer their feed in real time. To address this growing gap between how recommendations are optimized and how users wish to articulate their interests, we present Shape Your Feed (SYF), an LLM-based agentic recommendation framework that enables real-time, multimodal co-curation of content. SYF employs a three-tier architecture: (i) a Perception Flow that captures fine-grained user intent from text prompts, voice commands, and UI interactions; (ii) a Serving Flow that performs real-time agentic re-ranking and pruning of candidate items, grounded in a persistent Semantic Profile encoding evolving user preferences; and (iii) a Self-Evolution Flow that aligns system behavior with human judgments via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and an LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble. Offline evaluations show that SYF's alignment scoring module achieves 98.85% accuracy, substantially improving over strong few-shot baselines. Large-scale online A/B experiments on production traffic further demonstrate that SYF improves feed relevance and user sentiment, indicating a practical and scalable path toward interactive, user-steerable recommendation in industrial settings.

</details>


### [111] [TRACE: A Multi-Layer Benchmark for Human AI Controller Coordination Under Drift and Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06657)
*Joshua Zuniga,Srinivasan Subramanian,Ramya Madhuri Narapureddy,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个面向AI辅助网络物理系统中跨层漂移检测与归因的基准数据集（基于ALFRED生成1,918条漂移轨迹），设计了消除起始时间泄漏的诚实评估协议，并提供基线实验验证了漂移可被识别和归因，同时发现重型注意力模型在此类符号基准上并无优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络物理和AI辅助系统将人类操作者、AI决策模块和自动化控制器耦合在同一控制回路中，可信赖性取决于整个回路而非单一模型。然而现有基准无法捕获跨层、时间对齐的漂移传播轨迹，导致无法诊断协调故障的位置、原因及恢复方法。本文聚焦于漂移这一特定问题——它可能源自任意层级，且传统单模态监控无法将其定位到特定层或确定起始时间。

Method: 从ALFRED（日常家务任务 grounding-instruction 基准）导出轨迹，注入受控漂移，生成1,918条带漂移的轨迹。每条轨迹包含跨五个执行层（状态、观察、决策、规则、控制）的时间对齐的逐步记录，并标注漂移类型、受影响层、起始时间、责任主体和因果机制。标注由独立评审者验证并报告互评一致性。同时设计了一个"泄漏感知"协议，消除近乎完美的起始时间泄漏，并在经典、递归和注意力三类模型家族上进行基线研究。

Result: 在诚实协议下，所有模型家族的漂移识别与归因性能均显著高于随机和多数类基线：受影响层宏F1接近0.70，责任主体接近0.85，因果机制接近0.49。重型注意力模型在此类符号基准上相对简单模型并无优势。

Conclusion: 该基准和协议为跨层漂移诊断提供了标准化评估手段，表明漂移在符号化、多层的设置下可以被有效识别和归位，且复杂模型并非必然更好，为未来AI辅助系统的可信赖性研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern cyber-physical and AI-assisted systems couple human operators, AI decision modules, and automated controllers in a single control loop, so trustworthiness depends on the whole loop, not any one model. Yet no standard benchmark captures time-aligned, multi-layer traces of how drift and failures propagate across these layers, so we cannot diagnose where coordination breaks down, why, or how to recover. This paper targets one facet of that gap: drift, a deviation that can originate in any stack layer and that conventional single-modality monitoring cannot localize to a layer or pin to an onset time. We construct a benchmark by injecting controlled drift into traces derived from ALFRED, a grounded-instruction benchmark for everyday household tasks, yielding 1,918 drifted traces. Each trace is a time-aligned sequence of per-step records across five execution layers (state, observation, decision, rules, control), labeled with the drift type, affected layer, onset time, responsible actor, and causal mechanism, and validated by independent raters with inter-annotator agreement reported. We pair the dataset with a leak-aware protocol that removes a near-perfect onset leak, and a baseline study across classical, recurrent, and attention-based model families. Under this honest protocol, drift is identifiable and attributable well above random and majority baselines across every family (affected layer macro-F1 near 0.70, responsible actor near 0.85, causal mechanism near 0.49), and heavy attention offers no advantage over simpler models on this symbolic benchmark.

</details>


### [112] [CellWorld: From Gene-Level Reconstruction to Latent Cell Prediction in Spatial Transcriptomics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06659)
*Haiping Liu,Qian Zhao,Lijing Lin,Jingyuan Sun,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CellWorld，一种用于空间转录组的潜在空间预测预训练基础模型。与现有直接重建掩蔽基因身份或表达值的方法不同，CellWorld预测掩蔽细胞的潜在表示，避免重建检测特异性的技术变异。通过在4600万个人类细胞上预训练四个规模的变体（574万到9456万参数），实验表明CellWorld在11个线性探针基准和7个微调空间基准上均超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组基础模型主要通过重建掩蔽基因身份或表达值进行预训练，这种做法可能鼓励模型复制检测特异性的技术变异，从而限制了表征的可迁移性。作者希望避免直接重建这类变异，转而预测潜在细胞表征，以获得更具泛化能力的基础模型。

Method: CellWorld将预测目标从观测到的基因测量值转移到潜在细胞表征，从可见的空间上下文和有限的部分表达提示（partial-expression hint）中预测掩蔽细胞的潜在表示。作者在4600万人类细胞语料上预训练了四个规模的变体（574万至9456万可训练参数），并进行了受控的规模实验。

Result: 性能随模型容量提升而改善，尤其是在空间任务上。空间迁移更依赖于充分的优化和广泛的生物来源多样性，而非单纯的细胞数量。在四个保留数据集中，最小的CellWorld-Small（574万参数）在所有11个线性探针基准和7个微调空间基准上均超越所有基线。更值得关注的是，仅用语料库5%数据预训练的冻结CellWorld-Large，在全部7个空间基准上超越了所有全量微调基线。

Conclusion: 潜在空间预测预训练是构建空间转录组基础模型的可扩展有效路径，其表征迁移能力优于直接重建基因测量的方法，且模型性能可通过规模扩展和增加生物来源多样性进一步提升。

Abstract: This paper shows that latent-space predictive pretraining can provide a scalable route to foundation models for spatial transcriptomics. Existing spatial transcriptomics foundation models primarily reconstruct masked gene identities or expression values, potentially encouraging the reproduction of assay-specific technical variation and limiting representation transferability. To avoid directly reconstructing such variation, we shift the prediction target from observed gene measurements to latent cell representations and introduce CellWorld, which predicts the latent representations of masked cells from visible spatial context and a limited partial-expression hint. We pretrain four CellWorld variants, spanning 5.74M to 94.56M trainable parameters, on a corpus of 46 million human cells. Our controlled scaling experiments show that performance improves with model capacity, particularly on spatial tasks, while spatial transfer depends more on sufficient optimization and broad biological source diversity than on cell count alone. Across four held-out datasets, even CellWorld-Small, with 5.74M trainable parameters, outperforms every baseline on all 11 linear-probe benchmarks and all seven fine-tuned spatial benchmarks. Most notably, a frozen CellWorld-Large pretrained on only 5\% of the corpus with broad biological source coverage outperforms every fully fine-tuned baseline across all seven spatial benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/UoM-HealthAI/CellWorld.

</details>


### [113] [Vehicle routing problem using deep reinforcement learning - A case study about truck planning in the industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06668)
*Siliang Lu,Dan Hu,Lili Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于深度强化学习（DRL）的车辆路径优化在外部卡车网络设计中的实际工业应用，通过三个物流用例展示了DRL方法在VRP问题上的部署与优化效果，其优化后的路线相比基线方案降低了超过10%的总成本，并展望了DRL算法在未来更多VRP变体中的推广潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的VRP数学模型在真实工业场景中面临诸多挑战，包括复杂约束、信息不透明、不确定性以及非理性人类行为等，导致经典算法在实际部署中难以维持最优效果。因此需要探索新的方法来解决真实世界中VRP优化面临的这些难题。

Method: 本文通过三个不同的外部卡车网络设计的物流用例，将深度强化学习（DRL）算法应用于车辆路径优化问题。文章详细介绍了DRL-based车辆路径优化在实际工业场景中的具体实施流程和方法框架。

Result: 经过强化学习智能体优化的路线相比基线结果实现了超过10%的总成本降低，验证了DRL方法在真实工业物流场景中VRP优化的有效性。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习在车辆路径优化中展现出了显著的工业应用价值，未来研究可将DRL算法推广至更多VRP变体，以应对更广泛的路径优化需求。

Abstract: As an important component of the supply chain industry, transportation has experienced rapid development in the past decade with the assistance of digital platforms and intelligent algorithms. Within the field of transportation research, Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP) has remained a persistent and enduring challenge. In the realm of management science, experts, and scholars from both the industrial and academic sectors have continuously explored optimization models and algorithms to effectively address routing problems, from the classical Traveling Salesman Problem to the more general Vehicle Routing Problem. These models and algorithms are applied in real-world industrial scenarios to achieve cost optimization and reduce carbon footprints. However, due to the complexity of real-world problems, numerous specific constraints are often added, and challenges such as information opacity, uncertainty, and irrational human behavior may arise. Therefore, deploying and optimizing mathematical models for VRP in practical scenarios while maintaining optimal results poses numerous challenges. This paper discusses and provides solutions for three different logistic use cases involving external truck network design. Through these industrial case study, the paper introduces how deep reinforcement learning-based vehicle routing optimization has been implemented. As a result, it can be observed that the routes optimized by reinforcement learning agent have over 10% total cost compared to baseline results. Furthermore, the paper proposes that in future research, DRL algorithms for vehicle routing problems could be generalized into more variations of VRP.

</details>


### [114] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Coarse-Grained Molecular Dynamics of Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06694)
*Joohee Choi,Junhyeong Lee,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CGMas是一个多智能体框架，利用大语言模型从自然语言描述出发自动化完成聚合物的粗粒化建模全流程，包括拓扑构建、平衡、映射、势能推导和验证，在27项均聚物和共聚物任务中全部成功，并以1分钟速度达到与全原子密度5%以内的匹配精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自下而上的粗粒化建模过程劳动密集，因为粗粒化分辨率是设计选择，转移参数集通常不可用，且每次聚合物映射都需要重新推导势能函数，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 构建多智能体框架CGMas：LLM推理智能体从聚合物名称推断全原子拓扑，分层自校正机制解决不饱和键、杂原子和极性聚合物的常见物理错误；下游智能体依次负责系统平衡、映射到粗粒化表示、通过玻尔兹曼反演推导势能，并以全原子参考为基准验证模型。

Result: CGMas完成了全部27项均聚物和共聚物任务，其中22项的全原子密度匹配误差在5%以内，并将模拟时间从38-88分钟缩短至1分钟。

Conclusion: 智能体化大语言模型为实现自动化聚合物粗粒化提供了一条有效路径，展示了LLM在多尺度模拟自动化中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Coarse-grained (CG) molecular dynamics extends polymer simulation beyond the scales accessible to all-atom (AA) methods, but bottom-up CG modeling is laborious. The CG resolution is a design choice, so a transferable parameter set is generally not available and the potentials are derived anew for each polymer mapping. Here we present CGMas, a multi-agent framework that automates topology construction, equilibration, mapping, potential derivation, and validation from a natural-language specification of the polymer and target resolution. A large-language-model (LLM) reasoning agent infers the AA topology from polymer name, while layered self-correction resolves physical errors common to unsaturated, heteroatom-containing, and polar polymers. Downstream agents equilibrate the system, map it onto CG representation, derive potentials through Boltzmann inversion, and benchmark the model against its atomistic reference. CGMas completed all 27 homopolymer and copolymer tasks, matched the AA density to within 5% in 22, and reduced simulation from 38-88 min to 1 min, establishing agentic LLMs as a route to automated polymer coarse-graining.

</details>


### [115] [AgentPatch: Coarse-to-Fine Weak-Task Repair for Merging Agentic Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06699)
*Zibo Shao,Baochen Xiong,Chengdong Xu,Linhui Xiao,Kaichen Li,Haoran Gong,Yan Li,Yaguang Song,Xiaoshan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentPatch，一个面向智能体多模态大语言模型（Agentic MLLM）合并的训练-free粗到细修复框架，通过弱任务独特残差恢复和行为关键补丁来解决合并中的非对称能力保留与行为关键遗忘问题，生成单一静态检查点，无需路由或集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体多模态大语言模型虽然具备多模态感知、推理、规划、工具使用和交互能力，但现有模型往往针对特定工具或环境专门化，难以整合为一个通用模型。模型合并中存在两个关键挑战：(1) 非对称能力保留——不同交互复杂度的能力在合并后被不均匀地保留，导致合并后出现弱任务；(2) 行为关键遗忘——丢失决定性动作会破坏长时程执行流程。这两大问题阻碍了多模型能力的有效合并。

Method: AgentPatch包含三个阶段：(1) 选择稳定的合并骨干模型；(2) 通过Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery恢复被稀释的弱任务特有信号；(3) 应用Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch，在显式能力保护下恢复决定性行为。整个框架无需训练，采用粗到细的修复策略，最终生成单一静态检查点，无需路由机制或模型集成。

Result: 在六个智能体和多模态基准上的实验表明，AgentPatch能够改进多种合并骨干模型，缓解弱任务退化问题，并在弱任务恢复与互补的搜索、智能体视觉处理能力保留之间取得更好的平衡。

Conclusion: AgentPatch为智能体MLLM合并提供了一种无需训练的修复方案，有效解决了能力保留不均衡和关键行为遗忘问题，实现单检查点的通用模型合并。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend multimodal perception and reasoning with planning, tool use, and interaction in dynamic environments. Yet current models are specialized for particular tools or environments, complicating consolidation into a single generalist. We formulate Agentic MLLM Merging and identify two challenges: asymmetric capability preservation, whereby capabilities with different interaction complexity are retained unevenly, producing weak tasks after merging, and behavior-critical forgetting, whereby losing decisive actions can derail long-horizon execution. We propose AgentPatch, a training-free coarse-to-fine repair framework. It selects a stable merged backbone, restores diluted weak-task-specific signals through Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery, and applies an Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch that recovers decisive behaviors under explicit capability protection. AgentPatch produces a single static checkpoint without routing or ensembles. Experiments across six agentic and multimodal benchmarks show that AgentPatch improves diverse merged backbones, alleviates weak-task degradation, and better balances weak-task recovery with the preservation of complementary search and agentic visual processing capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/ziboshao/AgentPatch.

</details>


### [116] [WebRider: Persona-Conditioned Intent Controllers for Live-Web Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06704)
*Zhi Li,Tao Zhou,Yeqing Li,Eugene Ie,Demetri Terzopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WebRider框架，将网络任务委派形式化为"意图契约"（intent contract），解决现有实时网络Agent仅评估最终答案而忽略策略约束的关键缺陷。通过层级架构（控制器-中间层-工具层）实现可审计、可评判、可学习的浏览器浏览路径系统，并在4,096个实时网络契约基准RiderBench上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时网络Agent的评估仅关注最终答案，忽视了委派过程中的关键策略约束（如验证什么、如何处理不确定性、偏好和终止条件）。研究表明一个强控制器虽然能完成99.2%的任务，但仅38.8%的情况下遵守所有策略约束——"完成"不等于"忠实"。这暴露了现有评估范式的根本性缺陷。

Method: WebRider将委派策略形式化为意图契约——包含目标、约束、证据义务、答案形式和任务局部人格控制的运行时记录。采用层级架构：顶层控制器维护契约，中间层将意图实现为带守卫的可执行动作，工具层通过浏览器、搜索和地图工具执行动作。其RiderBench基准涵盖42个公开网站的4,096个实时契约，同时审计内部契约状态和可见用户体验。守卫中间接口还用作高质量训练信号，训练8B动作策略模型。

Result: WebRider的8B动作策略模型通过守卫中间接口训练后，在固定控制器下优于仅可执行行动的基线。RiderBench实现对内部契约状态和用户可见体验的双重审计，用于判定rollout是否保留策略以及步骤是否与人格一致。数据集已在hf.co/datasets/WebRider/WebRider公开。

Conclusion: 通过将浏览路径作为一等的可审计对象，WebRider实现了不混淆动作实现与最终答案决策的可审计、可人类评判、可学习的网络Agent系统。这弥补了从"完成任务"到"忠实委派"之间的关键鸿沟。

Abstract: Delegating a web task involves more than asking a question; it requires transferring a policy: what to verify, how to handle uncertainty, which preferences matter, and when to stop. Yet, current live-web agents are evaluated solely on the final answer, ignoring the policy constraints that define the delegation. A plausible final answer can conceal violations of that policy. Our full live audit reveals this critical gap: a strong controller completes 99.2% of tasks but honors all policy constraints in only 38.8% of cases. Finishing does not imply fidelity. WebRider bridges this gap by formalizing the delegated policy as an intent contract---an operational record of goals, constraints, evidence obligations, answer form, and task-local persona controls that must hold even as web pages change. WebRider employs a hierarchical architecture: a top-layer controller maintains the contract, a middle layer realizes intentions as guarded executable actions, and a tool layer executes these actions via browser, search, and maps tools. Our benchmark, RiderBench, evaluates this design on 4,096 live-web contracts across 42 public websites, auditing both the internal contract state and the visible user experience to determine if a rollout preserved its policy and if the steps were persona-consistent. The guarded middle interface also serves as a high-quality training signal; an 8B action-policy model trained through this interface outperforms executable-only baselines under a fixed controller. By making the browsing path a first-class object, WebRider enables a system that is auditable, human-judgeable, and learnable without conflating action realization with final-answer decisions. Dataset URL: hf.co/datasets/WebRider/WebRider.

</details>


### [117] [MolBioKG: Grounding Out-of-Graph Molecules in Biomedical Knowledge Graphs via Multi-Resolution Structural Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06713)
*Yiming Zhang,Hikaru Shindo,Shuan Chen,Kaushalya Madhawa,Jun Jin Choong,Yuna Oikawa,Takashi Fujiwara,Keisuke Ozawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MolBioKG是一个双层生物医学知识图谱系统，通过多分辨率结构锚定将未注册分子连接到大规模知识图谱中，解决"图外分子"冷启动问题，无需任务特定训练即可实现推理，在多项任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学知识图谱管线假设查询分子已作为图实体存在，导致未注册（图外）分子无法与KG交互，形成冷启动挑战。传统方法需要任务特定训练且缺乏可解释性，难以处理新出现的候选分子。

Method: MolBioKG采用双层架构：(1) 底层索引包含274万个分子，以骨架、片段、官能团和指纹等多分辨率结构表示；(2) 上层是960万边的生物医学知识图谱。给定SMILES字符串，系统通过多分辨率结构锚定检索结构相关的图实体，再遍历其生物医学邻域。推理机制包括：静态多锚点检索（基于倒数排名融合RRF）和Adapt-KG（一种使用工具的大型语言模型策略，实现自适应遍历）。

Result: 在图内链路恢复、复杂多跳推理和图外泛化任务中，MolBioKG均优于强基线。多跳推理中Hits@10从0.585提升到0.876，图外目标召回率从0.145提升到0.269，且所有预测都保留可追溯的结构锚点和带来源的知识图谱证据。

Conclusion: MolBioKG有效解决了生物医学KG中未注册分子的冷启动问题，通过多分辨率结构锚定实现了无需任务特定训练的通用推理框架，同时保证了预测的可解释性和证据可追溯性，为药物发现中的图外分子推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) accelerate drug discovery, but standard pipelines assume query molecules already exist as graph entities, leaving unregistered molecules disconnected. We address this cold-start challenge, termed the out-of-graph molecule problem, by introducing MolBioKG. This two-layer system grounds unseen molecules in biomedical evidence via multi-resolution structural anchoring. It connects an index of 2.74 million molecules (represented by scaffolds, fragments, functional groups, and fingerprints) to a 9.6-million-edge KG. Given only a SMILES string, MolBioKG retrieves structurally related graph entities and traverses their biomedical neighborhoods without task-specific training. It features two inference mechanisms: static multi-anchor retrieval using Reciprocal Rank Fusion, and Adapt-KG, a tool-using LLM policy for adaptive traversal. Evaluated across in-graph link recovery, complex multi-hop reasoning, and out-of-graph generalization, MolBioKG outperforms strong baselines. Notably, it raises Hits@10 from 0.585 to 0.876 in multi-hop reasoning and out-of-graph target recall from 0.145 to 0.269, all while ensuring predictions retain traceable structural anchors and source-attributed KG evidence.

</details>


### [118] [The Optimizer Is the Agent: Reasoning-Driven Search across Prompts, Programs, and ML Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06714)
*Junbo Li,Boyi Liu,Canwen Xu,Yite Wang,Yuxiong He,Zhangyang Wang,Qiang Liu,Zhewei Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ReASearch框架，将推理驱动的优化能力内化到单一工具使用智能体中，统一的智能体循环配合领域特定工具即可优化提示词、程序和机器学习工作流，在14个多样化任务上相比专门的优化系统取得2%-40%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词、程序和ML工作流优化系统通常依赖显式的外层控制器（如进化搜索、bandits或文本梯度方法），作者质疑这种搜索策略中有多少可以被单个工具使用智能体内部化，从而探索统一的智能体驱动优化范式。

Method: 提出ReASearch框架，采用统一的智能体循环配合领域特定工具。智能体自主决定评估什么、如何诊断失败、做出哪些编辑以及何时验证或重启；通过持久记忆在长时程内主动分析结果、分配预算并优化策略，而非仅作为由手工设计启发式引导的提案生成器。

Result: 在14个任务上与专门的优化系统相比具有竞争力且大部分情况下更好，相较于强领域特定基线取得2%-40%的提升，部分情况下发现了优于先前人类最佳已知结果的解决方案。观察到通常由显式控制器实现的复杂搜索行为会从智能体的推理过程中自然涌现。

Conclusion: ReASearch证明了一个共享的智能体脚手架配合领域特定工具即可取代显式外层搜索控制器，复杂搜索行为可涌现而非被显式编程，为推理驱动的统一优化开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Recent systems for optimizing prompts, programs, and ML workflows typically rely on explicit outer-loop controllers such as evolutionary search, bandits, or textual-gradient methods. We ask a fundamentally different question: how much of this search policy can be internalized by a single tool-using agent? We present ReASearch, a unified framework for reasoning-driven optimization in which the agent autonomously decides what to evaluate, how to diagnose failures, which edits to make, and when to verify or restart. Rather than serving only as a proposal generator guided by hand-designed heuristics, the agent actively analyzes outcomes, allocates budget, and refines its strategy over long horizons through persistent memory. With a shared agent loop and domain-specific tools, ReASearch instantiates the exact same scaffold to optimize prompts, programs, and ML workflows. Across 14 diverse tasks, it is competitive with and mostly better than specialized optimization systems, achieving gains of 2% to 40% over strong domain-specific baselines, and in some cases discovering solutions that improve on prior human best-known results. Crucially, we observe that complex search behaviors, which are typically implemented by explicit controllers, emerge naturally from the agent's reasoning process.

</details>


### [119] [bioMoR: Biology-Guided Mixture-of-Recursions for Effective Genomic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06727)
*Koushik Howlader,Tirtho Roy,Md Tauhidul Islam,Wei Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出bioMoR，这是首个将Mixture-of-Recursions (MoR)框架应用于基因级和通路级组学学习的框架。通过在图谱信息共享、结构化注意力偏置和图感知路由三个位置整合生物学知识，bioMoR在8个基准数据集上平均宏F1提升8.2个百分点、平衡准确率提升7.1个百分点，同时比非递归Transformer减少75%的参数和58%的FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维组学数据分析中，Transformer处理数千个基因或通路时计算开销巨大，但实际上只有部分token需要深层计算。现有的MoR框架虽然通过自适应token选择或专家选择路由提高了效率，但尚未在生物组学领域得到应用，也未整合领域特有的生物学结构知识。

Method: bioMoR在MoR骨干中确定三个整合结构化生物学知识的位置：(1) 基于图的信息共享用于精炼token嵌入；(2) 结构偏置引导自注意力聚焦于生物学相关的tokens；(3) 图感知路由器利用邻域信息决定每个token的递归深度。核心洞察是token间交互的额外知识能帮助模型构建嵌入并选择需要更深度学习的目标。

Result: 在涵盖多样组学数据类型的8个基准上，采用统一的五折交叉验证协议，bioMoR相比最强的生物学无关MoR基线平均宏F1提升8.2个百分点，平衡准确率提升7.1个百分点；相比非递归Transformer减少75%的参数和最多58%的FLOPs。选出的标记基因或通路具有生物学可解释性，token特定的递归深度揭示了计算资源的分配方式。

Conclusion: bioMoR验证了将生物学结构知识嵌入MoR框架能同时提升预测性能、计算效率和生物学可解释性。token级递归深度分析为理解计算分配提供了新视角，该方法在组学分析领域具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Transformer models for high-dimensional omics analysis process thousands of genes or pathways, although only a subset requires deep computation. Mixture-of-Recursions (MoR) improves efficiency through adaptive token-choice or expert-choice routing. We propose bioMoR, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first framework to apply MoR to gene-level and pathway-level learning. Our contributions include identifying three locations for integrating structured biological knowledge within an MoR backbone: graph-based information sharing refines token embeddings, a structural bias guides self-attention toward biologically related tokens, and a graph-aware router uses neighborhood information to determine each token's recursion depth. These techniques are centered on our insight that additional knowledge of token interaction can effectively help models construct embeddings and select which tokens should be learned more deeply. Across eight benchmarks spanning diverse omics data types and evaluated under a unified five-fold cross-validation protocol, bioMoR improves average macro-F1 by 8.2 percentage points and balanced accuracy by 7.1 percentage points over the strongest biology-agnostic MoR baseline while using 75 percent fewer parameters and up to 58 percent fewer FLOPs than a non-recursive Transformer. The selected marker genes or pathways provide biological interpretability, while their token-specific recursion depths reveal how computation is allocated.

</details>


### [120] [From Cheap Fakes to Pure Synthesis: Addressing the New Era of T2V Fake News Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06732)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Liang Lan,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对文本到视频（T2V）生成模型制造的纯合成虚假新闻视频检测问题，提出了一种新的三元分类任务（真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造），构建了首个纯合成虚假新闻视频数据集PS-FNVD，并提出了推理引导的R-T2V检测框架，该框架结合高层语义逻辑与低层物理生成痕迹，在10个基线模型上实现了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着T2V生成模型的快速发展，虚假新闻视频可以完全从零合成，超越了以往基于现有素材拼接的"廉价伪造"威胁。这类合成新闻视频与捏造叙事高度对齐，形成了"模态对齐陷阱"，使得现有检测器失效。现有数据集缺乏纯合成伪造新闻视频样本，而直接使用T2V模型生成的样本虽然对齐完美，但会将检测任务退化为单模态捷径问题，并导致语义-视觉退化。

Method: 本文提出：(1) 将T2V-FNVD形式化为新的三元分类任务（真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造），规避单模态捷径；(2) 构建首个纯合成虚假新闻视频数据集PS-FNVD，包含两个类型：捏造事件与对齐欺骗（Type 1）和真实事件但视觉来源虚假（Type 2）；(3) 提出推理引导的T2V-FNVD框架R-T2V，通过条件推理生成和有监督微调训练，整合高层语义逻辑与低层物理生成痕迹来预测三元真实性标签。

Result: 在10个主流基线模型上的广泛实验表明，R-T2V框架达到了最先进的性能，准确率比第二好的基线高出12.20个百分点，宏F1值高出8.46个百分点。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地解决了T2V时代纯合成虚假新闻视频检测的新挑战，通过三元分类任务设计、首个专用数据集构建以及推理引导的检测框架，有效克服了模态对齐陷阱和单模态捷径问题，显著提升了检测性能。

Abstract: Recent text-to-video (T2V) generation models enable fake news videos to be synthesized from scratch, shifting the threat beyond cheap fakes assembled from existing footage. Such news videos can closely match fabricated narratives, creating a modality alignment trap for existing detectors. Existing datasets lack pure synthesis fake news videos. Although directly prompting T2V models with descriptions of fake news videos can yield perfectly aligned samples, it reduces the fake news video detection (FNVD) to unimodal shortcuts and causes semantic-visual degeneration. To counter this, we formulate T2V-FNVD as a novel ternary classification task with three labels (real, cheap fake, and pure synthesis fake) and construct the first pure synthesis fake news video dataset (PS-FNVD). PS-FNVD includes fabricated events with aligned deception (Type 1) and true events with false visual provenance (Type 2), preventing models from exploiting unimodal shortcuts. Furthermore, we propose the Reasoning-guided T2V-FNVD (R-T2V) framework. Trained through conditioned rationale generation and supervised fine-tuning, R-T2V integrates high-level semantic logic with low-level physical generative traces to predict the ternary veracity label. Extensive experiments across 10 prevailing baselines show that R-T2V achieves the state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the second-best baseline by 12.20 percentage points in accuracy and 8.46 percentage points in macro $F_1$.

</details>


### [121] [IB-RL: Isolated Bilateral Reinforcement Learning for Strategic Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06735)
*Senhao Wang,Chenghao Cai,Haitao Hu,Mingxing Huang,Xingguang Wang,Wenhao Li,Zecheng Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出孤立双边强化学习（IB-RL）方法，通过让两个角色在联合环境中共同演化、各自独立优化奖励与优势函数，解决LLM在战略对话场景中因"静态对手不匹配"导致的策略泛化失败问题，在车辆电话销售和Deal-or-NoDeal两个任务上均显著超越单边RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法在训练LLM处理战略对话任务时，通常将目标智能体与固定的对手或模拟器训练，这会导致策略依赖对手特定规律而非学习通用策略，产生"静态对手不匹配"问题。战略对话的本质是双方互动的动态博弈，固定对手的训练环境无法反映这一特性。

Method: 提出孤立双边强化学习（IB-RL）：两个角色（谈判双方）通过联合rollout共同演化，每个角色拥有完全独立的优势函数、动作掩码和更新路径，各自优化自身奖励。这种严格的逐智能体隔离机制避免了训练过程中的信息泄漏，使策略更具泛化性。

Result: 在Vehicle TeleSales任务上，IB-RL达到89.6%的Success@1，优于最佳单边RL基线的84.6%；在Deal-or-NoDeal任务上，IB-RL对DeepSeek V4 Pro达到98.4%的协议率，相比最佳单边基线的86.4%有显著提升。

Conclusion: 在不可见对手的评估下，联合训练两个角色并保持严格的逐智能体隔离，能够使策略对未见对手具有更好的泛化能力，验证了双边共同演化训练在战略对话场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved strong results in improving large language models (LLMs) on tasks with stationary, verifiable rewards, such as mathematical reasoning and code execution.
